# Field and problem-family map — 13 October 2026

This is a navigation map of author claims, versions and bounded study results. No famous problem receives a solved label in this edition. Read [the rubric](SCHEMA.md), [major claims](MAJOR-CLAIMS.md), [scoped results](SCOPED-RESULTS.md), and [source audit](SOURCES.md) before interpreting a title as a theorem.

Coverage: 719 active manuscripts and 3 withdrawn manuscripts in 372 families across 17 fields. All active manuscript rows remain metadata-only. The separate 29 major-claim entries comprise 25 statement/scope/notice reviews and 4 catalog-only entries; 21 local scoped-result entries receive additional treatment. These counts do not measure solved problems.

| Field | Families | Active manuscripts |
|---|---:|---:|
| Number theory | 31 | 58 |
| Algebraic and complex geometry | 36 | 86 |
| Real and complex analysis | 16 | 26 |
